Model before rewriting:

letting a be {2,3}
find b: set (size 2) of int(1..3)

such that

b = a

--

b
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b
new variables:
  find b#packed_1: int(0..7)
new constraints:
  sum([(b#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 2

--

b#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1
new variables:
  find b#packed_1#rank_1: bool
  find b#packed_1#rank_2: bool
  find b#packed_1#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(b#packed_1#rank_3),or([!(b#packed_1#rank_2),or([!(b#packed_1#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

b#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1

--

b#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1

--

b = {2,3}
   ~~> eq_packed_literal ([("ReprGeneral", 9500)])
b#packed_1 = 6

--

b#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1

__0

__0,
sum([(b#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 2,
true
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])

true
